id="data-title">Data ingestion (structured files)</h2>\n        <p class="sub">Read a CSV/TSV or XLSX file into a 
typed table with a provenance pointer. Post the file to\n          <code>/api/ingest</code>. PDF, scans and SPSS files 
are refused: <strong>no PDF text extraction or OCR</strong>\n          is implemented.</p>\n        <div

class="panel" id="panel-check" hidden aria-labelledby="check-title">\n      <div class="card">\n        <h2 
id="check-title">Check my trial balance</h2>\n        <p class="muted">Paste your trial balance straight from Excel: 
one account per line, with its debit or\n          credit. Nothing is calculated on this page &mdash; the engine 
compares what you give it with the\n          trial balance your own journal produces, and tells you where the two 
disagree.</p>\n        <label class="field" for="tbinput">Your trial balance &mdash; account, debit, credit (tab, 
comma or semicolon)</label>\n        <textarea id="tbinput" rows="12" spellcheck="false" 
placeholder="Cash&#9;1500&#9;&#10;Capital&#9;&#9;1500"></textarea>\n        <div class="row" 
style="margin-top:10px">\n          <button class="btn primary" id="checkrun" type="button">Check</button>\n          
<button class="btn" id="checkdemo" type="button">Use an example</button>\n          <button class="btn" 
id="checkclear" type="button">Clear</button>\n        </div>\n        <p class="muted" style="margin-top:10px">With 
only a trial balance I can check that it balances and is\n          internally consistent. That is the weakest check 
in accounting: errors of principle, commission,\n          original entry, omission, reversal and compensating errors 
all leave a trial balance balanced.</p>\n      </div>\n      <div id="checkout"></div>\n    </section>\n

id="dhelp">\n      <h3>What this is</h3>\n      <p class="sub">A local workbench that calls the local API. Every 
number shown is computed by the method library\n        and printed exactly as the server returned it. Nothing is 
calculated in the browser.</p>\n      <h3>How to work</h3>\n      <p class="sub">1. Describe the question on Home and 
run it, or pick a subject in the rail.<br>\n        2. Read the work plan and the frozen checks, then confirm.<br>\n   
     3. Read the result with its honesty notes and limitations.<br>\n        4. Save it as a case; cases keep 
versions, comparisons and sign-offs.</p>\n      <h3>Offline</h3>\n      <p class="sub">No external network calls, no 
web fonts, no analytics.</p>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</aside>\n\n<div class="toasts" id="toasts"

function api(path, opts) {\n  const res = await fetch(path, Object.assign({ headers: { "Content-Type": 
"application/json" } }, opts || {}));\n  let data = null;\n  try { data = await res.json(); } catch (e) { data = null; 
}\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error((data && data.error) || ("The server returned HTTP " + res.status + "."));\n  return

a.dataset.subject === state.subjectId);\n    if (on) a.setAttribute("aria-current", "page"); else 
a.removeAttribute("aria-current");\n  });\n  document.title = "Smart BusinessWriter - " + (id === "subject" && 
state.subjectId ? state.subjectId : id);\n  const target = (id === "subject" && state.subjectId)\n    ? "#/subject/" + 
encodeURIComponent(state.subjectId)\n    : (id === "case" && state.caseId)\n      ? "#/case/" + 
encodeURIComponent(state.caseId)\n      : "#/" + id;\n  if (location.hash !== target) history.replaceState(null, "", 
target);\n}\nfunction show(id) {\n  activate(id);\n  const render = RENDERERS[id];\n  if (render) 
render();\n}\nfunction route() {\n  const raw = (location.hash || "#/home").replace(/^#\\/?/, "");\n  const parts = 
raw.split("/").filter(Boolean);\n  const head = parts[0] || "home";\n  if (head === "subject" && parts[1]) {\n    
state.subjectId = decodeURIComponent(parts[1]);\n    state.subject = null;\n    show("subject");\n    return;\n  }\n  
if (head === "case" && parts[1]) {\n    state.caseId = decodeURIComponent(parts[1]);\n    show("case");\n    return;\n 
 }\n  show(PANELS.includes(head) ? head : "home");\n}\n\n/* ---------- panels ---------- */\nconst RENDERERS = 
{};\n\nRENDERERS.home = function () {\n  const p = $("#panel-home");\n  p.innerHTML =\n    \'<div class="hero">\' +\n  
    \'<img src="\' + MARK + \'" alt="">\' +\n      \'<h1>Smart Business <span class="g">Writer</span></h1>\' +\n      
\'<p class="tagline">BUSINESS | ACCOUNTING | ECONOMICS | FINANCE</p>\' +\n      \'<p class="slogan">Your reliable 
business writing assistant.<br>Real computation. Real research. Verifiable.</p>\' +\n    "</div>" +\n    \'<div 
class="actionbox">\' +\n      \'<label class="visually-hidden" for="objective">What would you like to work 
on?</label>\' +\n      \'<textarea id="objective" placeholder="What would you like to work on..."></textarea>\' +\n    
  \'<div class="actionrow">\' +\n        \'<button class="btn" id="attachbtn" type="button" title="Attach files for 
this analysis">Attach files</button>\' +\n        \'<input type="file" id="attachfile" multiple hidden>\' +\n        
\'<button class="runbtn" id="run" type="button" aria-label="Start the analysis" title="Start the analysis (Enter)">\' 
+\n          \'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 19V5M6 11l6-6 6 6"/></svg>\' +\n        
"</button>" +\n      "</div>" +\n      \'<div id="attachments" class="attachments" aria-live="polite"></div>\' +\n    
"</div>";\n  $("#run").onclick = startFromHome;\n  $("#objective").addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {\n    if (e.key 
=== "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing) { e.preventDefault(); startFromHome(); }\n  });\n  bindAttachments(p);\n 
 loadAttachments();\n};\n\n/* ---------- intake: attached files ---------- */\nfunction fileSize(bytes) {\n  const n = 
Number(bytes);\n  if (!isFinite(n) || n < 0) return "size not recorded";\n  if (n < 1024) return n + " B";\n  if (n < 
1024 * 1024) return (n / 1024).toFixed(1) + " KB";\n  return (n / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + " MB";\n}\n\nfunction 
readAsBase64(file) {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const reader = new FileReader();\n    
reader.onload = () => {\n      const text = String(reader.result || "");\n      const comma = text.indexOf(",");\n     
 resolve(comma >= 0 ? text.slice(comma + 1) : "");\n    };\n    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error || new 
Error("the file could not be read"));\n    reader.readAsDataURL(file);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function 
uploadAttachments(files) {\n  const list = Array.from(files || []);\n  if (!list.length) return;\n  const box = 
$("#attachments");\n  if (box) box.innerHTML = loading("Storing " + list.length + " file" + (list.length === 1 ? "" : 
"s"));\n  let stored = 0;\n  for (const file of list) {\n    try {\n      const content_base64 = await 
readAsBase64(file);\n      await post("/api/intake", { filename: file.name, content_base64: content_base64 });\n      
stored += 1;\n    } catch (error) {\n      toast(file.name + ": " + (error.message || String(error)));\n    }\n  }\n  
if (stored) toast(stored === 1 ? "File attached." : stored + " files attached.");\n  loadAttachments();\n}\n\nfunction 
intakeStatus(row) {\n  if (row.corrupt) return \'<span class="badge warn">record unreadable</span>\';\n  if 
(row.status === "ingested") return \'<span class="badge">Ingested</span>\';\n  if (row.status === "reference_only") 
return \'<span class="badge mute">Reference only</span>\';\n  if (row.error) return \'<span class="badge warn">Stored, 
not read</span>\';\n  return \'<span class="badge mute">Stored</span>\';\n}\n\nfunction intakeDetail(row) {\n  if 
(row.corrupt) return \'<div class="muted">\' + esc(row.error) + "</div>";\n  if (row.error) return \'<div 
class="muted">\' + esc(row.error) + "</div>";\n  if (row.status === "ingested") {\n    const cols = (row.columns || 
[]).join(", ");\n    return \'<div class="muted">\' + esc(row.n_rows) + " rows x " + esc(row.n_cols) + " columns" +\n  
    (cols ? \' <details><summary>Columns read</summary>\' + esc(cols) + "</details>" : "") + "</div>";\n  }\n  return 
\'<div class="muted">\' + esc(row.note) + "</div>";\n}\n\nfunction intakeRow(row) {\n  const id = row.id || "";\n  
const actions =\n    (row.kind === "structured" && row.status !== "ingested"\n      ? \'<button class="btn sm" 
type="button" data-ingest="\' + esc(id) + \'">Ingest</button>\' : "") +\n    \'<button class="btn sm" type="button" 
data-remove="\' + esc(id) + \'">Remove</button>\';\n  return \'<div class="attachrow"><div 
class="attachmeta"><strong>\' + esc(row.filename || id) + "</strong>" +\n    \'<div class="muted">\' + 
esc(fileSize(row.bytes)) + " - " + intakeStatus(row) + "</div>" +\n    intakeDetail(row) + \'</div><div class="row">\' 
+ actions + "</div></div>";\n}\n\nasync function loadAttachments() {\n  const box = $("#attachments");\n  if (!box) 
return;\n  try {\n    const data = await api("/api/intake");\n    const rows = (data && data.files) || [];\n    if 
(!rows.length) {\n      box.innerHTML = \'<p class="muted">No files attached. Spreadsheets are read into a typed 
table; \' +\n        "any other file is stored and labelled not read.</p>";\n      return;\n    }\n    box.innerHTML = 
rows.map(intakeRow).join("");\n    $$("[data-ingest]", box).forEach((b) => { b.onclick = () => 
ingestAttachment(b.dataset.ingest); });\n    $$("[data-remove]", box).forEach((b) => { b.onclick = () => 
removeAttachment(b.dataset.remove); });\n  } catch (error) {\n    box.innerHTML = errorBanner(error);\n  }\n}\n\nasync 
function ingestAttachment(id) {\n  try {\n    await post("/api/intake/" + encodeURIComponent(id) + "/ingest", {});\n   
 toast("File read into a typed table.");\n  } catch (error) {\n    toast(error.message || String(error));\n  }\n  
loadAttachments();\n}\n\nasync function removeAttachment(id) {\n  try {\n    await api("/api/intake/" + 
encodeURIComponent(id), { method: "DELETE" });\n  } catch (error) {\n    toast(error.message || String(error));\n  }\n 
 loadAttachments();\n}\n\nfunction bindAttachments(panel) {\n  const button = $("#attachbtn"), input = 
$("#attachfile"), box = panel && panel.querySelector(".actionbox");\n  if (button && input) {\n    button.onclick = () 
=> input.click();\n    input.onchange = (event) => { uploadAttachments(event.target.files); event.target.value = ""; 
};\n  }\n  if (!box) return;\n  ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((name) => box.addEventListener(name, (event) => {\n  
  event.preventDefault(); box.classList.add("dragging");\n  }));\n  ["dragleave", "dragend"].forEach((name) => 
box.addEventListener(name, () => box.classList.remove("dragging")));\n  box.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {\n    
event.preventDefault(); box.classList.remove("dragging");\n    if (event.dataTransfer && event.dataTransfer.files) 
uploadAttachments(event.dataTransfer.files);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction subjectCard(row) {\n  return \'<button 
class="subjectcard" type="button" data-subject="\' + esc(row.id) + \'">\' +\n    \'<span class="shield"><span 
class="capicon" style="color:\' + toneOf(row.id) + \'">\' + iconSvg(row.icon) + "</span>" +\n      \'<span 
class="sctitle">\' + esc(row.label) + "</span></span>" +\n    \'<span class="scdesc">\' + esc(row.summary) + "</span>" 
+\n    \'<span class="meta">\' +\n      \'<span class="badge">\' + esc(row.tool_count) + " calculations</span>" +\n    
  (row.worksheet_count ? \'<span class="badge mute">\' + esc(row.worksheet_count) + " worksheets</span>" : "") +\n     
 \'<span class="badge mute">\' + esc(row.catalogue_count) + " catalogue</span>" +\n      (row.cross_cutting ? \'<span 
class="badge warn">cross-cutting</span>\' : "") +\n    "</span>" +\n    \'<span class="capgo">Open the 
capabilities</span>\' +\n  "</button>";\n}\n\nfunction bindSubjectCards(root) {\n  $$("[data-subject]", 
root).forEach((b) => { b.onclick = () => openSubject(b.dataset.subject); });\n}\n\n/* One source of truth for the rail 
and the shelf: the product\'s subject index. */\nfunction ensureSubjects() {\n  if (state.subjects &&

inputs");\n  const level = (!isSheet && item.level)\n    ? \'<span class="lvl">\' + esc(item.level) + "</span>"\n    :

"";\n  return \'<button class="capcard" type="button" \' + attrs + ">" +\n    \'<span class="caphead"><span 
class="capicon" style="color:\' + toneOf(state.subjectId) + \'">\' + iconSvg(toolIcon(isSheet ? { kind: "worksheet" }

class="capdesc">\' + esc(item.what) + "</span>" +\n    \'<span class="capfoot"><span>\' + footer + \'</span><span 
class="capgo">\' +\n      (isSheet ? "Open worksheet" : "Use this tool") + " &rarr;</span></span>" +\n  
"</button>";\n}\n\nfunction renderSubject() {\n  const s = state.subject; if (!s) return;\n  const out = 
$("#subjectout");\n  let html = \'<div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start">\' +\n 
   \'<div class="shield"><span class="capicon" style="width:44px;height:44px;color:\' + toneOf(s.id) + \'">\' +\n      
iconSvg(s.icon) + \'</span><div><h2 style="margin:0">\' + esc(s.label) + \'</h2>\' +\n      \'<p class="sub"

subjects</button></div>\' +\n    \'<p class="muted" style="margin-top:6px">Catalogue disciplines: \' +

statistics methods</button>\' : "") +\n    "</div>";\n  html += \'<div class="capgroup"><h3 
style="margin-top:0">Choose what to do in \' + esc(s.label) + "</h3>" +\n    \'<p class="muted">Each tile opens the 
tool with the method the server will run. Nothing is calculated until you \' +\n    "supply or confirm the figures on 
the next screen.</p>";\n  const tools = s.tools || [];\n  const sheets = (s.worksheets || []).map((w) => 
capabilityCard(w, "worksheet"));\n  const levels = [["intro", "Intro"], ["intermediate", "Intermediate"], ["advanced", 
"Advanced"]];\n  const groups = [];\n  tools.forEach((t) => {\n    if (!t.group) return;\n    let g = groups.find((x) 
=> x.label === t.group);\n    if (!g) { g = { label: t.group, items: [] }; groups.push(g); }\n    g.items.push(t);\n  
});\n  const hasGroups = tools.length && tools.every((t) => t.group);\n  const hasLevels = tools.length && 
tools.every((t) => t.level);\n  if (hasGroups) {\n    groups.forEach((g) => {\n      html += \'<h4 
class="levelhead">\' + esc(g.label) + \'</h4><div class="capgrid">\' +\n        g.items.map((t) => capabilityCard(t, 
"compute")).join("") + "</div>";\n    });\n  } else if (hasLevels) {\n    levels.forEach((pair) => {\n      const 
subset = tools.filter((t) => t.level === pair[0]);\n      if (subset.length) {\n        html += \'<h4 
class="levelhead">\' + pair[1] + \'</h4><div class="capgrid">\' +\n          subset.map((t) => capabilityCard(t, 
"compute")).join("") + "</div>";\n      }\n    });\n  } else if (tools.length) {\n    html += \'<div 
class="capgrid">\' + tools.map((t) => capabilityCard(t, "compute")).join("") + "</div>";\n  }\n  if (sheets.length) 
{\n    html += \'<h4 class="levelhead">Worksheets</h4><div class="capgrid">\' + sheets.join("") + "</div>";\n  }\n  if 
(!tools.length && !sheets.length) {\n    html += \'<p class="muted">No computed tool or worksheet is assigned to this 
subject yet.</p>\';\n  }\n  html += "</div>";\n  if (s.worksheets.length) {\n    html += \'<p class="muted">Worksheets 
are structure and evidence audit only: they score and rank nothing, and the output says so.</p>\';\n  }\n  html +=

html += \'<h3>Catalogue only - no worksheet or method yet</h3>\' +\n    \'<p class="muted">These records are readable 
structures. The implementation status is the registry\\\'s own wording, copied verbatim; nothing here is presented as

tools.filter((t) => t.formula);\n  if (noted.length) {\n    html += \'<h3>Study notes</h3><p class="muted">Formula, 
steps and the mistakes students most often make. \' +\n      \'The numbers still come from the frozen method, not from

t.common_mistakes.map((x) => "<li>" + esc(x) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>" : "") +\n        (t.diagram ? \'<p 
class="muted">Diagram to draw: \' + esc(String(t.diagram).replace(/_/g, " ")) + ".</p>" : "") +\n        ((t.related

"</details>";\n    });\n  }\n  out.innerHTML = html;\n  $("#subjectback").onclick = () => show("subjects");\n  const

(home) home.onclick = () => show("home");\n  bindSubjectCards(out);\n  $$("[data-tool]", out).forEach((b) => {\n

};\n  });\n  $$("[data-worksheet]", out).forEach((b) => {\n    b.onclick = () => openWorksheet(b.dataset.worksheet,

function openTool(tool) {\n  state.tool = tool;\n  const preset = tool.preset ? state.presets[tool.preset] : null;\n  
const body = {\n    objective: (preset && preset.objective) || tool.label,\n    method: tool.method,\n    inputs: 
preset ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(preset.inputs)) : {}\n  };\n  if (preset && preset.currency) body.currency = 
preset.currency;\n  if (preset && preset.period) body.period = preset.period;\n  state.tool = Object.assign({}, tool, 
{ inputs: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(body.inputs)) });\n  await createSession(body);\n}\n\nasync function 
openWorksheet(template, label) {\n  state.tool = { kind: "worksheet", label: label || template, method: template, 
preset: null };\n  try {\n    const t = await api("/api/templates/" + encodeURIComponent(template));\n    const req = 
t.request || t.example || {};\n    const body = { objective: req.objective || ("Work through the " + (label || 
template) + " worksheet."),\n                   method: template, inputs: req.inputs || {} };\n    state.session = 
await post("/api/sessions", body);\n    show("new");\n    renderNew();\n  } catch (e) {\n    
$("#panel-subject").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e));\n  }\n}\n\n\nRENDERERS.calculator = function () 
{\n  const note = $("#calcnote"), out = $("#calcout");\n  note.innerHTML = loading("Reading the frozen method 
list");\n  out.innerHTML = "";\n  api("/api/cases/calculator").then((d) => {\n    state.calculator = d;\n    if 
(!d.groups || !d.groups.length) {\n      note.innerHTML = banner("blocked", "No calculations returned", "<div>The 
server returned an empty tool list. Nothing has been invented here.</div>");\n      return;\n    }\n    
state.calcGroup = state.calcGroup || d.groups[0].id;\n    note.innerHTML = banner("honesty", "What this Calculator 
is", "<div>" + esc(d.note) + "</div>");\n    $("#calcgroups").innerHTML = d.groups.map((g) =>\n      \'<button 
class="chip" data-calcgroup="\' + esc(g.id) + \'"><span class="dot"></span>\' + esc(g.label) + 
"</button>").join("");\n    $$("[data-calcgroup]").forEach((b) => { b.onclick = () => { state.calcGroup = 
b.dataset.calcgroup; renderCalculator(); }; });\n    $("#calcq").oninput = renderCalculator;\n    
renderCalculator();\n  }).catch((e) => { note.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); });\n};\n\nfunction renderCalculator() {\n  
const d = state.calculator; if (!d) return;\n  const group = d.groups.find((g) => g.id === state.calcGroup) || 
d.groups[0];\n  const q = ($("#calcq").value || "").trim().toLowerCase();\n  $$("[data-calcgroup]").forEach((b) => {\n 
   const on = b.dataset.calcgroup === group.id;\n    b.style.borderColor = on ? "var(--navy-300)" : "";\n    
b.style.background = on ? "var(--navy-100)" : "";\n  });\n  const tools = group.tools.filter((t) =>\n    !q || 
(t.label + " " + t.method + " " + t.what).toLowerCase().includes(q));\n  $("#calcout").innerHTML = tools.length\n    ? 
"<h3>" + esc(group.label) + " - " + esc(group.tools.length) + " calculations</h3>" +\n      tools.map((t) => \'<div 
class="toolrow"><div class="tooltext"><strong>\' + esc(t.label) + "</strong>" +\n        "<span>" + esc(t.what) + " 
<em>Method: " + esc(t.method) + (t.preset ? ", preset " + esc(t.preset) : ", typed inputs") + "</em></span></div>" +\n 
       \'<button class="btn sm" data-calctool="\' + esc(t.key) + \'">Use this calculation</button></div>\').join("")\n 
   : \'<p class="muted">No calculation in this group matches that search.</p>\';\n  $$("[data-calctool]").forEach((b) 
=> {\n    const tool = group.tools.find((t) => t.key === b.dataset.calctool);\n    b.onclick = () => { if (tool) 
openTool(tool); };\n  });\n}\n\n\nasync function startFromHome() {\n  state.tool = null;\n  const objective = 
$("#objective").value.trim();\n  if (!objective) { toast("Describe the question first."); return; }\n  // The Home box 
no longer carries method controls (owner instruction: logo + chat box only).\n  // Method selection lives in the 
subject rail and the Calculator.\n  const methodSelect = $("#method");\n  const method = methodSelect ? 
methodSelect.value : "";\n  if (!method) { toast("Choose a subject from the rail, or open the Calculator, to pick a 
method."); return; }\n  const presetSelect = $("#preset");\n  const preset = presetSelect ? 
state.presets[presetSelect.value] : null;\n  const body = { objective: objective, method: method };\n  const inputs = 
preset ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(preset.inputs)) : {};\n  body.inputs = inputs;\n  const currencySelect = 
$("#currency");\n  const currency = currencySelect ? currencySelect.value : "";\n  if (currency) body.currency = 
currency;\n  if (preset && preset.period) body.period = preset.period;\n  state.tool = { label: method, method: 
method, preset: presetSelect ? presetSelect.value || null : null,\n                 inputs: 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inputs)) };\n  await createSession(body);\n}\n\nfunction ensurePanel(id) {\n  let p = 
document.getElementById("panel-" + id);\n  if (!p) {\n    // the panel was removed from the markup by a layout change; 
recreate it rather than fail\n    p = document.createElement("section");\n    p.className = "panel";\n    p.id = 
"panel-" + id;\n    p.hidden = true;\n    (document.getElementById("main") || document.body).appendChild(p);\n    if 
(PANELS.indexOf(id) === -1) PANELS.push(id);\n  }\n  return p;\n}\n\nasync function createSession(body) {\n  const p = 
ensurePanel("new");\n  state.session = null;\n  p.innerHTML = "<div class=\\"card\\">" + loading("Creating the 
session") + "</div>";\n  show("new");\n  try {\n    state.session = await post("/api/sessions", body);\n    
state.delivery = state.session.delivery || null;\n    renderNew();\n  } catch (e) { p.innerHTML = "<div 
class=\\"card\\">" + errorBanner(e) + "</div>"; }\n}\n\nRENDERERS.new = function () {\n  if (state.session) { 
renderNew(); return; }\n  const p = ensurePanel("new");\n  p.hidden = false;\n  p.innerHTML = \'<div 
class="card"><h2>No session yet</h2>\' +\n    \'<p class="sub">A session lives in this browser tab. Reloading the page 
starts a fresh one, so this page has nothing to show.</p>\' +\n    \'<div class="row"><button class="btn primary" 
id="newsession">Start from a subject</button>\' +\n    \'<button class="btn" id="newcases">Open a saved 
case</button></div></div>\';\n  const b = $("#newsession"); if (b) b.onclick = () => show("subjects");\n  const c = 
$("#newcases"); if (c) c.onclick = () => show("cases");\n};\n\nfunction inputValue(name, spec, plan) {\n  if 
(state.tool && state.tool.inputs && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.tool.inputs, name)) {\n    return 
state.tool.inputs[name];\n  }\n  const recorded = plan && plan.inputs && plan.inputs[name];\n  let value = recorded ? 
recorded.value : "";\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";\n  if ((spec && spec.type) === 
"decimal_list" && typeof value === "string") {\n    const m = value.trim().match(/^\\[(.*)\\]$/s);\n    if (m) {\n     
 return m[1].split(",").map((x) => x.trim().replace(/^[\'"]|[\'"]$/g, "")).filter((x) => x.length);\n    }\n    return 
value.split(",").map((x) => x.trim()).filter((x) => x.length);\n  }\n  return value;\n}\n\nfunction inputField(name, 
spec, plan) {\n  const type = (spec && spec.type) || "text";\n  const desc = type === "engagement_spec"\n    ? "what 
the engine will post: the period, the currency and framework, the chart, the opening balances, the entries and any 
schedules"\n    : ((spec && spec.desc) || "");\n  const value = inputValue(name, spec, plan);\n  const label = type 
=== "engagement_spec" ? "The engagement" : name;\n  if (type === "decimal_list") {\n    const text = 
Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(", ") : String(value || "");\n    return \'<div class="stack" style="gap:4px"><label 
class="field" for="in-\' + esc(name) + \'">\' + esc(name) +\n      \' <span class="muted">\' + esc(desc) + 
"</span></label>" +\n      \'<input type="text" id="in-\' + esc(name) + \'" data-type="decimal_list" value="\' + 
esc(text) + \'" placeholder="comma separated"></div>\';\n  }\n  if (type === "object" || type === "array" || type === 
"engagement_spec" ||\n      (value !== null && typeof value === "object")) {\n    const text = typeof value === 
"string" ? value : JSON.stringify(value || (type === "array" ? [] : {}), null, 1);\n    const rows = Math.min(16, 
Math.max(4, text.split("\\n").length + 1));\n    return \'<div class="stack" style="gap:4px"><label class="field" 
for="in-\' + esc(name) + \'">\' + esc(label) +\n      \' <span class="muted">\' + esc(desc) + "</span></label>" +\n    
  \'<textarea id="in-\' + esc(name) + \'" data-type="object" rows="\' + rows + \'" spellcheck="false" \' +\n      
\'class="jsoninput" placeholder="{}">\' + esc(text) + "</textarea>" +\n      (type === "engagement_spec"\n        ? 
\'<p class="muted" style="margin:4px 0 0">Change a date, an amount or an entry. Every entry must balance: \' +\n       
   "its debits must equal its credits, or the engine will refuse the figures and say which entry is out.</p>"\n        
: "") + "</div>";\n  }\n  if (type === "boolean") {\n    return \'<div class="row"><input type="checkbox" id="in-\' + 
esc(name) + \'" data-type="boolean"\' + (value ? " checked" : "") +\n      \'><label class="field" for="in-\' + 
esc(name) + \'">\' + esc(name) + \' <span class="muted">\' + esc(desc) + "</span></label></div>";\n  }\n  return 
\'<div class="stack" style="gap:4px"><label class="field" for="in-\' + esc(name) + \'">\' + esc(name) +\n    \' <span 
class="muted">\' + esc(desc) + "</span></label>" +\n    \'<input type="text" id="in-\' + esc(name) + \'" 
data-type="decimal" value="\' + esc(value) + \'"></div>\';\n}\n\nfunction renderNew() {\n  const s = state.session;\n  
if (!s) { $("#panel-new").innerHTML = \'<div class="empty">No session yet.</div>\'; return; }\n  const plan = 
s.work_plan || {};\n  const method = state.methods.find((m) => m.method_id === (plan.method || s.spec && 
s.spec.method));\n  const schema = (method && method.input_schema) || {};\n  const delivered = s.delivery && 
s.delivery.status === "delivered";\n  const blocked = s.delivery && s.delivery.status === "blocked";\n  const mapping 
= s.status === "awaiting_mapping_confirmation";\n  let html = \'<div class="card">\' +\n    "<h2>" + (state.tool ? 
"Tool - " + esc(state.tool.label) : "New analysis") + "</h2>" +\n    \'<div class="row" 
style="justify-content:space-between"><span class="sub">\' + esc(plan.objective || "") + "</span>" +\n    \'<span 
class="badge">\' + esc(plan.tier_label || txt(plan.tier)) + "</span></div>" +\n    \'<div class="row" 
style="margin:8px 0 2px"><button class="btn sm" id="toolback">Back to subjects</button>\' +\n      \'<button 
class="btn sm" id="toolcalc">Calculator</button></div>\' +\n    "</div>";\n\n  if (!delivered) {\n    html += \'<div 
class="card" id="figures">\' +\n      (blocked ? banner("blocked", "The engine refused these figures", "<div>" + 
esc(s.delivery.blocked_reason) +\n          "</div><p class=\\"muted\\" style=\\"margin:8px 0 0\\">Correct the figure 
below and run it again.</p>") : "") +\n      (mapping ? \'<h2>The account mapping</h2>\' +\n        \'<p 
class="sub">The spec is frozen and the accounts are mapped. This is the second checkpoint: confirm the mapping \' +\n  
      \'and the engine posts the entries, then builds the statements. Nothing is posted before you confirm.</p>\' +\n  
      \'<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 6px"><button class="btn primary" id="confirmmap">Confirm the account 
mapping and post</button>\' +\n        \'<button class="btn" id="reject">Reject</button></div>\' +\n        \'<p 
class="muted" style="margin:0 0 12px">Session \' + esc(s.id) + " - " + esc(s.status) + "</p>" +\n        \'<pre 
class="json">\' + esc(JSON.stringify(s.account_mapping || {}, null, 1)) + "</pre>"\n      : \'<h2>Enter the 
figures</h2>\' +\n        \'<p class="sub">These are the figures the engine will post. The engagement is declared 
once: the period, the chart, \' +\n        \'the opening balances, the entries and any schedules. Change a figure, 
then run it.</p>\' +\n        \'<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 6px"><button class="btn primary" id="apply">Apply 
figures and calculate</button>\' +\n        \'<button class="btn" id="reject">Reject</button></div>\' +\n        \'<p 
class="muted" style="margin:0 0 12px">Session \' + esc(s.id) + " - " + esc(s.status) + "</p>" +\n        
(Object.keys(schema).length\n          ? \'<div class="grid2">\' + Object.keys(schema).map((n) => inputField(n, 
schema[n], plan)).join("") + "</div>"\n          : \'<p class="muted">This method declares no inputs.</p>\')) +\n      
"</div>";\n  }\n\n  html += \'<div class="card"><h2>Method</h2>\' +\n    kv([["Method", (method && method.method_id) 
|| plan.method], ["Version", method && method.version],\n        ["Tier", plan.tier], ["Review status", method && 
method.review_status],\n        ["Intended use", method && method.intended_use], ["Unsupported use", method && 
method.unsupported_use]]) +\n    "</div>";\n  if (s.spec) {\n    html += \'<div class="card"><h2>Draft work 
plan</h2>\' +\n      kv([["Spec hash", s.spec.hash || "not frozen yet - the spec is hashed when you confirm"],\n       
   ["Method version", plan.method_version], ["Planned checks", (plan.planned_checks || []).join(", ")]]) +\n      
(plan.ambiguities && plan.ambiguities.length\n        ? "<h3>Assumptions and ambiguities</h3>" + 
plan.ambiguities.map((a) =>\n            \'<div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:6px"><strong>\' + esc(a.question) + 
"</strong><br>" +\n            esc(a.default_chosen) + "<br>" + esc(a.impact) + "</div>").join("")\n        : "") +\n  
    (plan.inputs ? "<h3>Inputs as recorded</h3>" + kv(Object.keys(plan.inputs).map((k) =>\n            [k, 
(plan.inputs[k] && plan.inputs[k].provenance) + ": " + (plan.inputs[k] && plan.inputs[k].value)])) : "") +\n      
"</div>";\n  }\n  if (s.delivery) {\n    html += \'<div class="card"><h2>Stage status (from run events)</h2>\' +\n     
 kv(Object.keys(s.stage_states || {}).map((k) => [k, s.stage_states[k]])) +\n      \'<pre class="json" 
style="margin-top:12px">\' + esc(JSON.stringify(s.events, null, 1)) + "</pre></div>";\n  }\n  const suggested = 
(plan.framework_suggestions || {});\n  const suggestionRows = []\n    .concat((suggested.frameworks || []).map((x) => 
["Framework", x.name || x.id, x.why]))\n    .concat((suggested.quantitative || []).map((x) => ["Quantitative", x.name 
|| x.id, x.why]))\n    .concat((suggested.theories || []).map((x) => ["Theory", x.name || x.id, x.why]))\n    
.concat((suggested.named || []).map((x) => ["Named by you", x.name || x.id, x.why]));\n  if (suggestionRows.length) 
{\n    html += \'<div class="card"><h2>Suggested frameworks (informational)</h2>\' +\n      \'<p 
class="muted">Computed from your objective by the catalogue. Nothing is applied unless you confirm it in the frozen 
spec.</p>\' +\n      suggestionRows.map(([kind, name, why]) => \'<div class="toolrow"><div class="tooltext"><strong>\' 
+ esc(txt(name)) +\n        " <span class=\\"badge mute\\">" + esc(kind) + "</span></strong><span>" + esc(txt(why)) + 
"</span></div></div>").join("") +\n      (suggested.none_reason ? \'<p class="muted">\' + esc(suggested.none_reason) + 
"</p>" : "") + "</div>";\n  } else if (suggested.none_reason) {\n    html += \'<div class="card"><h2>Suggested 
frameworks (informational)</h2><p class="muted">\' + esc(suggested.none_reason) + "</p></div>";\n  }\n  
$("#panel-new").innerHTML = html;\n  const back = $("#toolback"); if (back) back.onclick = () => show(state.subjectId 
? "subject" : "subjects");\n  const tocalc = $("#toolcalc"); if (tocalc) tocalc.onclick = () => show("calculator");\n  
const apply = $("#apply"); if (apply) apply.onclick = applyAndRun;\n  const rj = $("#reject"); if (rj) rj.onclick = () 
=> confirmSession(true);\n  const cm = $("#confirmmap"); if (cm) cm.onclick = () => 
confirmSession(false);\n}\n\nfunction collectInputs(session) {\n  const s = session || state.session; if (!s) return 
null;\n  const method = state.methods.find((m) => m.method_id === ((s.work_plan || {}).method || (s.spec && 
s.spec.method)));\n  const schema = (method && method.input_schema) || {};\n  const inputs = {};\n  for (const name of 
Object.keys(schema)) {\n    const el = document.getElementById("in-" + name); if (!el) continue;\n    const type = 
el.dataset.type;\n    if (type === "boolean") { inputs[name] = el.checked; continue; }\n    const raw = 
el.value.trim(); if (!raw) continue;\n    if (type === "decimal_list") inputs[name] = raw.split(",").map((x) => 
x.trim()).filter((x) => x.length);\n    else if (type === "object") { try { inputs[name] = JSON.parse(raw); } catch 
(e) { toast("That field is not valid JSON."); return null; } }\n    else inputs[name] = raw;\n  }\n  return 
inputs;\n}\n\nasync function applyAndRun() {\n  const s = state.session; if (!s) return;\n  const inputs = 
collectInputs(s);\n  if (inputs === null) return;\n  const plan = s.work_plan || {};\n  const body = { objective: 
plan.objective, method: plan.method, inputs: inputs };\n  if (plan.currency) body.currency = plan.currency;\n  if 
(plan.period) body.period = plan.period;\n  if (state.tool) state.tool.inputs = inputs;\n  try {\n    let current = 
await post("/api/sessions", body);\n    if (current.status === "awaiting_confirmation") {\n      current = await 
post("/api/sessions/" + current.id + "/confirm", {});\n    }\n    state.session = current;\n    state.delivery = 
current.delivery || null;\n    if (current.delivery && current.delivery.status === "delivered") { show("results"); 
renderResults(); }\n    else { renderNew(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); }\n  } catch (e) { 
$("#panel-new").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); }\n}\n\nasync function redraft() { await 
applyAndRun(); }\n\nasync function confirmSession(reject) {\n  const s = state.session; if (!s) return;\n  try {\n    
state.session = await post("/api/sessions/" + s.id + "/" + (reject ? "reject" : "confirm"), {});\n    state.delivery = 
state.session.delivery || null;\n    if (!reject && state.session.delivery && state.session.delivery.status === 
"delivered") { show("results"); renderResults(); }\n    else { renderNew(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); }\n  } catch (e) { 
$("#panel-new").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); }\n}\n\nRENDERERS.results = function () { 
renderResults(); };\nfunction renderResults() {\n  const s = state.session; const d = s && s.delivery;\n  const p = 
$("#panel-results");\n  if (!d) { p.innerHTML = \'<div class="empty"><h3>No result yet</h3><p>Run an analysis 
first.</p></div>\'; return; }\n  if (d.status === "blocked") {\n    p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card">\' + 
banner("blocked", "Blocked", "<div>" + esc(d.blocked_reason) + "</div>") + "</div>";\n    return;\n  }\n  const ap = 
d.appendix || {};\n  const honesty = ap.honesty || {};\n  const honestyKeys = Object.keys(honesty).filter((k) => 
honesty[k]);\n  let html = \'<div class="card">\' +\n    \'<div class="row" 
style="justify-content:space-between"><h2>Result</h2><span class="badge">\' + esc(ap.tier || "") + "</span></div>" +\n 
   \'<div class="row" style="margin:0 0 12px">\' +\n      \'<button class="btn primary" id="savecase">Save as 
case</button>\' +\n      \'<button class="btn" id="tocharts">Charts and exports</button>\' +\n      \'<span 
class="grow"></span><span class="muted">Spec hash \' + esc(ap.spec_hash) + "</span></div>" +\n    \'<div 
class="report">\' + esc(d.report) + "</div>";\n  if (honestyKeys.length) {\n    html += banner("honesty", "Honesty: 
what was not done", "<ul>" + honestyKeys.map((k) =>\n      "<li>" + esc(k) + ": " + esc(typeof honesty[k] === "string" 
? honesty[k] : JSON.stringify(honesty[k])) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>");\n  }\n  if ((ap.limitations || []).length) 
{\n    html += banner("limit", "Limitations (as recorded)", "<ul>" + ap.limitations.map((l) => "<li>" + esc(l) + 
"</li>").join("") + "</ul>");\n  }\n  if ((ap.assumptions || []).length) {\n    html += banner("limit", "Assumptions 
(as recorded)", "<ul>" + ap.assumptions.map((l) => "<li>" + esc(l) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>");\n  }\n  html += 
"</div>";\n  html += \'<div class="card"><h3>Artifacts</h3>\' + artifactTable(ap) + "</div>";\n  html += \'<div 
class="card"><h3>Recorded metadata</h3>\' + kv([\n    ["Method", ap.method && ap.method.id], ["Method version", 
ap.method && ap.method.version],\n    ["Category", ap.method && ap.method.category], ["Tier", ap.tier], ["Spec hash", 
ap.spec_hash],\n    ["Code hash", ap.code_hash], ["Environment hash", ap.env_hash], ["Run id", ap.run_id],\n    
["Sandbox", ap.sandbox && ap.sandbox.isolation ? ap.sandbox.isolation : ap.sandbox],\n    ["Checks", (ap.tests || 
[]).join(", ")]]) + "</div>";\n  p.innerHTML = html;\n  $("#savecase").onclick = saveCase;\n  $("#tocharts").onclick = 
() => { state.caseId = state.savedCaseId || null; show("deliver"); };\n  window.scrollTo(0, 0);\n}\n\nfunction 
artifactTable(ap) {\n  const arts = ap.artifacts || [];\n  const outs = (ap.reperform && ap.reperform.outputs) || 
{};\n  if (!arts.length) return \'<p class="muted">This delivery recorded no artifacts.</p>\';\n  let html = \'<div 
class="tablewrap"><table><caption>Artifacts recorded by the delivery</caption><thead><tr>\' +\n    
"<th>Artifact</th><th>Metric</th><th>Value</th><th>Derivation</th></tr></thead><tbody>";\n  arts.forEach((a) => {\n    
const id = Array.isArray(a) ? a[0] : a.id;\n    const labels = Array.isArray(a) ? a[1] : null;\n    const metric = 
Array.isArray(a) ? (labels && labels[0]) : a.metric;\n    const derivation = Array.isArray(a) ? a[2] : a.derivation;\n 
   const value = outs[id] !== undefined ? outs[id] : (Array.isArray(a) ? null : a.value);\n    html += "<tr><td>" + 
esc(txt(id)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(metric)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(value)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(derivation)) + 
"</td></tr>";\n  });\n  return html + "</tbody></table></div>";\n}\n\nasync function saveCase() {\n  const s = 
state.session; if (!s || !s.delivery) return;\n  const ap = s.delivery.appendix || {};\n  const bundle = {\n    title: 
(s.work_plan || {}).objective || "Untitled case",\n    method: (s.work_plan || {}).method || (ap.method && 
ap.method.id) || "",\n    tier: ap.tier || "",\n    spec_hash: ap.spec_hash || "",\n    outputs_hash: (ap.reperform && 
ap.reperform.outputs_hash) || "",\n    session: s,\n    delivery: { report: s.delivery.report, appendix: ap },\n    
reperform: ap.reperform || {}\n  };\n  try {\n    const stored = await post("/api/cases", bundle);\n    state.caseId = 
stored.id; state.savedCaseId = stored.id;\n    toast("Case " + stored.id + " saved.");\n    openCase(stored.id);\n  } 
catch (e) { $("#panel-results").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); }\n}\n\n/* ---------- cases 
---------- */\nRENDERERS.cases = function () {\n  const p = $("#panel-cases");\n  p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card">\' 
+ loading("Reading the case store") + "</div>";\n  api("/api/cases").then((d) => {\n    state.cases = (d.cases || 
[]).slice().sort((a, b) =>\n      String(b.created_at || "").localeCompare(String(a.created_at || "")));\n    let html 
= \'<div class="card"><div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"><h2>Cases</h2>\' +\n      \'<div 
class="row"><button class="btn" id="backup">Back up all cases</button></div></div>\';\n    if (!state.cases.length) 
{\n      html += \'<div class="empty"><h3>No cases stored yet</h3><p>Run an analysis and press "Save as 
case".</p></div>\';\n    } else {\n      html += \'<div class="tablewrap"><table><caption>Stored 
cases</caption><thead><tr>\' +\n        
"<th>Title</th><th>Method</th><th>Tier</th><th>Created</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>";\n      
state.cases.forEach((c) => {\n        html += "<tr" + (c.corrupt ? \' class="row-warn"\' : "") + "><td>" + 
esc(txt(c.title)) +\n          (c.corrupt ? \' <span class="badge warn">corrupt</span>\' : "") + "</td><td>" + 
esc(txt(c.method)) +\n          "</td><td>" + esc(txt(c.tier)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(c.created_at)) + "</td>" +\n    
      \'<td><button class="btn sm" data-open="\' + esc(c.id) + \'">Open</button></td></tr>\';\n      });\n      html 
+= "</tbody></table></div>";\n    }\n    html += "</div>";\n    p.innerHTML = html;\n    $$("[data-open]").forEach((b) 
=> { b.onclick = () => openCase(b.dataset.open); });\n    const bk = $("#backup"); if (bk) bk.onclick = backupCases;\n 
 }).catch((e) => { p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card">\' + errorBanner(e) + "</div>"; });\n};\n\nasync function 
backupCases() {\n  try {\n    const r = await post("/api/cases/backup", {});\n    toast("Backup written to " + (r.path 
|| "the state directory") + ".");\n  } catch (e) { $("#panel-cases").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); 
}\n}\n\nfunction accountingBlocks(acc) {\n  if (!acc || typeof acc !== "object") return "";\n  let html = "";\n  const 
st = acc.statements_export || {};\n  const order = ["trial_balance", "income_statement", "balance_sheet", "cash_flow", 
"reconciliation", "notes"];\n  const parts = order.filter((k) => typeof st[k] === "string" && st[k].trim());\n  if 
(parts.length) {\n    html += "<h3>The statements</h3>" + parts.map((k) =>\n      "<h4>" + esc(k.replace(/_/g, " ")) + 
"</h4>" + \'<pre class="json statement">\' + esc(st[k]) + "</pre>").join("");\n  }\n  const checks = acc.checks || 
[];\n  if (checks.length) {\n    html += "<h3>The checks</h3><ul>" + checks.map((c) =>\n      "<li><strong>" + (c[1] ? 
"PASS" : "FAIL") + "</strong> &mdash; " + esc(c[0]) +\n      (c[2] ? " <span class=\\"muted\\">" + esc(c[2]) + 
"</span>" : "") + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>";\n  }\n  const t = acc.t_accounts_export || {};\n  const codes = 
Object.keys(t).sort();\n  if (codes.length) {\n    html += "<h3>The T-accounts</h3>" + codes.map((c) => \'<pre 
class="json statement">\' + esc(t[c]) + "</pre>").join("");\n  }\n  if (typeof acc.journal_export === "string" && 
acc.journal_export.trim()) {\n    html += "<h3>The journal</h3>" + \'<pre class="json statement">\' + 
esc(acc.journal_export) + "</pre>";\n  }\n  return html;\n}\nRENDERERS.case = function () { openCase(state.caseId); 
};\nasync function openCase(id) {\n  if (!id) return;\n  state.caseId = id;\n  state.caseBundle = null;\n  const p = 
$("#panel-case");\n  p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card">\' + loading("Reading the case") + "</div>";\n  
activate("case");\n  try {\n    state.caseBundle = await api("/api/cases/" + id);\n    const versions = await 
api("/api/cases/" + id + "/versions");\n    state.versions = versions.versions || [];\n    const signoffs = await 
api("/api/cases/" + id + "/signoff");\n    state.signoffs = signoffs.signoffs || [];\n    renderCase();\n  } catch (e) 
{ p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card">\' + errorBanner(e) + "</div>"; }\n}\n\nfunction renderCase() {\n  const b = 
state.caseBundle; if (!b) return;\n  const d = b.delivery || {}; const ap = d.appendix || {};\n  const honesty = 
ap.honesty || {};\n  const honestyKeys = Object.keys(honesty).filter((k) => honesty[k]);\n  let html = \'<div 
class="card">\' +\n    \'<div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"><h2>\' + esc(txt(b.title)) + "</h2>" 
+\n    \'<span class="badge">\' + esc(txt(b.tier)) + "</span></div>" +\n    \'<div class="row"><span 
class="muted">Case \' + esc(b.id) + " - " + esc(txt(b.created_at)) + " - " + esc(txt(b.method)) + "</span>" +\n    
\'<span class="grow"></span><button class="btn sm danger" id="delcase">Delete case</button></div>\' +\n    \'<div 
class="tabs" role="tablist">\' +\n      ["report", "versions", "comparison", "signoff"].map((t) =>\n        \'<button 
class="tab" role="tab" data-tab="\' + t + \'" aria-selected="\' + (state.tab === t) + \'">\' +\n        ({ report: 
"Report", versions: "Versions", comparison: "Comparison", signoff: "Sign-off" })[t] + "</button>").join("") +\n    
"</div>";\n  html += \'<div class="tabpanel" data-panel="report"\' + (state.tab === "report" ? "" : " hidden") + ">" 
+\n    \'<div class="row" style="margin:12px 0"><button class="btn primary" id="dlxlsx" type="button">Download the 
figures (Excel)</button>\' +\n    \'<button class="btn" id="dlword" type="button">Download the submission 
(Word)</button>\' +\n    \'<button class="btn" id="dlhtml" type="button">Download it as HTML (prints to 
PDF)</button>\' +\n    \'<span class="muted" id="dlmsg"></span></div>\' +\n    \'<div class="report">\' + 
esc(d.report) + "</div>" +\n    (ap.accounts ? accountingBlocks(ap.accounts) : "") +\n    (honestyKeys.length ? 
banner("honesty", "Honesty: what was not done", "<ul>" + honestyKeys.map((k) =>\n      "<li>" + esc(k) + ": " + 
esc(typeof honesty[k] === "string" ? honesty[k] : JSON.stringify(honesty[k])) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>") : "") +\n 
   ((ap.limitations || []).length ? banner("limit", "Limitations (as recorded)", "<ul>" + ap.limitations.map((l) => 
"<li>" + esc(l) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>") : "") +\n    "<h3>Artifacts</h3>" + artifactTable(ap) +\n    
\'<h3>Recorded metadata</h3>\' + kv([["Spec hash", ap.spec_hash], ["Outputs hash", b.outputs_hash],\n      ["Method", 
ap.method && ap.method.id], ["Method version", ap.method && ap.method.version],\n      ["Tier", ap.tier], ["Code 
hash", ap.code_hash], ["Environment hash", ap.env_hash], ["Run id", ap.run_id]]) +\n    "</div>";\n  html += \'<div 
class="tabpanel" data-panel="versions"\' + (state.tab === "versions" ? "" : " hidden") + ">" +\n    \'<div 
class="row"><button class="btn" id="newver">Save the current session as a new version</button></div>\' +\n    
(state.versions.length\n      ? \'<div class="tablewrap" style="margin-top:12px"><table><caption>Stored 
versions</caption><thead><tr>\' +\n        "<th>Version</th><th>Created</th><th>Method</th><th>Tier</th><th>Bundle 
hash</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>" +\n        state.versions.map((v) => "<tr><td>" + esc(v.version) + "</td><td>" 
+ esc(txt(v.created_at)) + "</td><td>" +\n          esc(txt(v.method)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(v.tier)) + "</td><td>" 
+ esc(txt(v.bundle_hash)) +\n          \'</td><td><button class="btn sm" data-ver="\' + esc(v.version) + 
\'">Open</button></td></tr>\').join("") +\n        "</tbody></table></div>"\n      : \'<div class="empty">No versions 
stored yet.</div>\') + "</div>";\n  html += \'<div class="tabpanel" data-panel="comparison"\' + (state.tab === 
"comparison" ? "" : " hidden") + ">" +\n    \'<div class="row"><button class="btn" id="cmp">Compare the selected 
versions</button><span class="muted" id="cmpnote"></span></div>\' +\n    \'<div id="cmpout"></div></div>\';\n  html += 
\'<div class="tabpanel" data-panel="signoff"\' + (state.tab === "signoff" ? "" : " hidden") + ">" +\n    \'<div 
class="grid2"><div class="stack"><label class="field" for="sgnrev">Reviewer name</label><input type="text" 
id="sgnrev">\' +\n    \'<label class="field" for="sgnrole">Role</label><input type="text" id="sgnrole">\' +\n    
\'<label class="field" for="sgnscope">Scope</label><input type="text" id="sgnscope" value="the case bundle and its 
disclosed limitations">\' +\n    \'<label class="field" for="sgnver">Version</label><input type="text" id="sgnver" 
value="1">\' +\n    \'<button class="btn primary" id="sgnsave">Record sign-off</button></div>\' +\n    \'<div 
id="sgnlist"></div></div></div>\';\n  html += "</div>";\n  $("#panel-case").innerHTML = html;\n  
$$("[data-tab]").forEach((t) => { t.onclick = () => { state.tab = t.dataset.tab; renderCase(); }; });\n  const del = 
$("#delcase"); if (del) del.onclick = () => confirmDelete(b.id);\n  const download = (kind, deliverable) => async () 
=> {\n    const msg = $("#dlmsg"); if (msg) msg.textContent = "Preparing the file...";\n    try {\n      const r = 
await post("/api/cases/" + encodeURIComponent(b.id) + "/export",\n                           { kind: kind, 
deliverable: deliverable, download: true });\n      const bin = atob(r.data), bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);\n    
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);\n      const url = URL.createObjectURL(new 
Blob([bytes], { type: r.mime }));\n      const a = document.createElement("a"); a.href = url; a.download = 
r.filename;\n      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();\n      setTimeout(() => 
URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);\n      if (msg) msg.textContent = "Saved " + r.filename + (r.credits && 
r.credits.charged ? " (1 credit)" : " (free)");\n    } catch (e) { if (msg) msg.textContent = e.message; }\n  };\n  
const x = $("#dlxlsx"), w = $("#dlword"), h = $("#dlhtml");\n  if (x) x.onclick = download("xlsx", "workpapers");\n  
if (w) w.onclick = download("docx", "submission");\n  if (h) h.onclick = download("html", "submission");\n  const nv = 
$("#newver"); if (nv) nv.onclick = newVersion;\n  const cm = $("#cmp"); if (cm) cm.onclick = compareVersions;\n  const 
sv = $("#sgnsave"); if (sv) sv.onclick = recordSignoff;\n  $$("[data-ver]").forEach((btn) => { btn.onclick = () => 
openVersion(Number(btn.dataset.ver)); });\n  renderSignoffs();\n  window.scrollTo(0, 0);\n}\n\nasync function 
newVersion() {\n  const s = state.session;\n  if (!s || !s.delivery) { toast("Run an analysis first, then save it as a 
version."); return; }\n  const ap = s.delivery.appendix || {};\n  const bundle = { title: (s.work_plan || 
{}).objective || "Untitled case", method: (s.work_plan || {}).method || "",\n    tier: ap.tier || "", spec_hash: 
ap.spec_hash || "", outputs_hash: (ap.reperform && ap.reperform.outputs_hash) || "",\n    session: s, delivery: { 
report: s.delivery.report, appendix: ap }, reperform: ap.reperform || {} };\n  try { const v = await 
post("/api/cases/" + state.caseId + "/versions", bundle); toast("Version " + v.version + " stored."); 
openCase(state.caseId); }\n  catch (e) { $("#panel-case").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); 
}\n}\n\nasync function openVersion(n) {\n  try {\n    const doc = await api("/api/cases/" + state.caseId + 
"/versions/" + n);\n    const b = doc.bundle || doc;\n    state.caseBundle = b; state.tab = "report"; renderCase();\n  
  toast("Version " + n + " loaded.");\n  } catch (e) { $("#panel-case").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", 
errorBanner(e)); }\n}\n\nasync function compareVersions() {\n  const out = $("#cmpout");\n  const picks = 
$$("#panel-case input[data-pick]:checked").map((i) => i.value);\n  const versions = picks.length ? picks.join(",") : 
state.versions.slice(0, 2).map((v) => v.version).join(",");\n  if (!versions) { out.innerHTML = \'<p class="muted">No 
versions to compare.</p>\'; return; }\n  out.innerHTML = loading("Comparing on the server");\n  try {\n    const cmp = 
await api("/api/cases/" + state.caseId + "/compare?versions=" + encodeURIComponent(versions));\n    state.compare = 
cmp;\n    let h = "";\n    if (cmp.issues && cmp.issues.length) h += banner("blocked", "Comparison issues", "<ul>" + 
cmp.issues.map((i) => "<li>" + esc(i) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>");\n    if (cmp.comparability) h += \'<p 
class="muted">\' + esc(cmp.comparability.rule) + "</p>";\n    h += \'<div class="tablewrap"><table><caption>Comparison 
(values copied from the stored bundles)</caption><thead><tr><th>Metric</th>\' +\n      (cmp.columns || []).map((c) => 
"<th class=\\"num\\">v" + esc(c.version) + "</th>").join("") + "</tr></thead><tbody>";\n    (cmp.rows || 
[]).forEach((r) => {\n      h += "<tr><td>" + esc(txt(r.metric)) + "</td>" + (cmp.columns || []).map((c) => {\n        
const v = r.values ? r.values[String(c.version)] : null;\n        const missing = (r.not_computed || 
[]).indexOf(c.version) >= 0;\n        return \'<td class="num">\' + (missing ? \'<span class="muted">not 
computed</span>\' : esc(txt(v))) + "</td>";\n      }).join("") + "</tr>";\n    });\n    h += 
"</tbody></table></div>";\n    if (cmp.note) h += \'<p class="muted">\' + esc(cmp.note) + "</p>";\n    out.innerHTML = 
h;\n  } catch (e) { out.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); }\n}\n\nfunction renderSignoffs() {\n  const box = $("#sgnlist");

case yet.</p>\'; return; }\n  box.innerHTML = state.signoffs.map((s) => \'<div class="card"><h3>Version \' + 
esc(txt(s.version)) + "</h3>" +\n    kv([["Reviewer", s.reviewer && s.reviewer.name], ["Role", s.reviewer &&

"</div>").join("");\n}\n\nasync function recordSignoff() {\n  const body = { version: Number($("#sgnver").value), 
reviewer: $("#sgnrev").value, role: $("#sgnrole").value,\n                 scope: $("#sgnscope").value, decision: 
"reviewed" };\n  try { await post("/api/cases/" + state.caseId + "/signoff", body); toast("Sign-off recorded."); 
openCase(state.caseId); }\n  catch (e) { $("#panel-case").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", errorBanner(e)); 
}\n}\n\nfunction confirmDelete(id) {\n  openModal("Delete this case?", "<p>The stored bundle, its versions and its 
sign-offs are removed from the local case store.</p>",\n    "Delete", async () => {\n      try { await 
api("/api/cases/" + id, { method: "DELETE" }); toast("Case deleted."); show("cases"); }\n      catch (e) { 
toast(e.message); }\n    });\n}\n\n/* ---------- data ---------- */\nRENDERERS.data = function () {\n  const p = 
$("#panel-data");\n  p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card"><h2>Data ingestion (structured files)</h2>\' +\n    \'<p 
class="sub">Read a CSV/TSV or XLSX file into a typed table with a provenance pointer. \' +\n    "Post the file to 
<code>/api/ingest</code>. PDF, scans and SPSS files are refused: " +\n    "<strong>no PDF text extraction or 
OCR</strong> is implemented.</p>" +\n    \'<div class="row"><input type="file" id="ingfile" 
accept=".csv,.tsv,.txt,.xlsx,.xlsm">\' +\n    \'<button class="btn primary" id="ingestbtn">Ingest file</button>\' +\n  
  \'<label class="field" style="margin:0">Sheet <input type="text" id="ingsheet" placeholder="name or 
number"></label>\' +\n    \'<label class="field" style="margin:0">Merged cells <select id="ingmerged"><option 
value="refuse">refuse</option>\' +\n    \'<option value="top_left">top_left</option></select></label></div>\' +\n    
\'<div id="ingout"></div></div>\' +\n    \'<div class="card"><h3>Map columns to a method</h3><p class="sub">The 
mapping is validated by the method itself; the server returns the mapped request.</p>\' +\n    \'<div 
class="row"><select id="mapmethod"><option value="">method</option>\' +\n      state.methods.map((m) => "<option 
value=\\"" + esc(m.method_id) + "\\">" + esc(m.method_id) + "</option>").join("") +\n    \'</select><input type="text"

id="mapbtn">Map columns</button></div><div id="mapout"></div></div>\';\n  $("#ingestbtn").onclick = ingestFile;\n  
$("#mapbtn").onclick = mapColumns;\n};\n\nasync function ingestFile() {\n  const input = $("#ingfile");\n  const out = 
$("#ingout");\n  if (!input.files || !input.files.length) { toast("Choose a file first."); return; }\n  const file = 
input.files[0];\n  out.innerHTML = loading("Reading the file");\n  const reader = new FileReader();\n  reader.onload = 
async () => {\n    const bytes = new Uint8Array(reader.result);\n    let binary = ""; for (let i = 0; i < 
bytes.length; i++) binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);\n    const body = { filename: file.name, content_base64: 
btoa(binary) };\n    const sheet = $("#ingsheet").value.trim(); if (sheet) body.sheet = sheet;\n    const merged = 
$("#ingmerged").value; if (merged) body.merged = merged;\n    try {\n      const r = await post("/api/ingest", 
body);\n      state.ingest = r;\n      renderIngest(r);\n    } catch (e) { out.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); }\n  };\n  
reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);\n}\n\nfunction renderIngest(r) {\n  const t = r.table || {};\n  let h = \'<div 
class="card"><h3>\' + esc(txt(t.filename)) + " (" + esc(txt(t.kind)) + ")</h3>" +\n    kv([["Rows", t.n_rows], 
["Columns", t.n_cols], ["SHA-256", t.sha256], ["Delimiter", t.delimiter]]) +\n    \'<div class="tablewrap" 
style="margin-top:12px"><table><caption>Columns as read</caption><thead><tr>\' +\n    
"<th>Column</th><th>Type</th><th>Present</th><th>Missing</th><th>Sample</th></tr></thead><tbody>" +\n    (t.columns || 
[]).map((c) => "<tr><td>" + esc(txt(c.name)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(c.type)) + "</td><td>" +\n      
esc(txt(c.n_present)) + "</td><td>" + esc(txt(c.n_missing)) + "</td><td>" + esc((c.sample || []).join(", ")) + 
"</td></tr>").join("") +\n    "</tbody></table></div>";\n  if (t.rows) {\n    h += \'<div class="tablewrap" 
style="margin-top:12px"><table><caption>Rows as read</caption><thead><tr>\' +\n      (t.columns || []).map((c) => 
"<th>" + esc(txt(c.name)) + "</th>").join("") + "</tr></thead><tbody>" +\n      t.rows.slice(0, 50).map((row) => 
"<tr>" + row.map((v) => "<td>" + esc(txt(v)) + "</td>").join("") + "</tr>").join("") +\n      
"</tbody></table></div>";\n  }\n  if (t.provenance) h += \'<h3>Provenance</h3>\' + 
kv(Object.keys(t.provenance).map((k) => [k, t.provenance[k]]));\n  if (t.not_read_as) h += \'<p class="muted">\' + 
esc(t.not_read_as) + "</p>";\n  h += "</div>";\n  $("#ingout").innerHTML = h;\n}\n\nasync function mapColumns() {\n  
const out = $("#mapout");\n  let columns;\n  try { columns = JSON.parse($("#mapcols").value || "{}"); }\n  catch (e) { 
out.innerHTML = errorBanner(new Error("That mapping is not valid JSON.")); return; }\n  try {\n    const r = await 
post("/api/ingest", { filename: state.ingest ? state.ingest.table.filename : "",\n      content_base64: state.ingest ? 
state.ingest.table.content_base64 : "", map: { method: $("#mapmethod").value, columns: columns } });\n    
out.innerHTML = \'<h3>Mapped request (as returned)</h3><pre class="json">\' + esc(JSON.stringify(r.mapped_request, 
null, 1)) + "</pre>";\n  } catch (e) { out.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); }\n}\n\n/* ---------- references ---------- 
*/\nRENDERERS.refs = function () {\n  const p = $("#panel-refs");\n  p.innerHTML = \'<div class="card"><h2>References

.sub").insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", "<br>" + esc(notices.harvard));\n  }).catch(() => {});\n  $("#verify").onclick 
= verifyReference;\n  $("#cite").onclick = getCitation;\n  $("#showlog").onclick = showLog;\n  $("#drift").onclick = 
checkDrift;\n};\n\nasync function verifyReference() {\n  const out = $("#refout");\n  out.innerHTML = 
loading("Verifying the reference");\n  try {\n    const r = await post("/api/literature/verify", { title: 
$("#reftitle").value, doi: $("#refdoi").value, source: $("#refsrc").value });\n    state.ref = r;\n    let h = "";\n   
 if (r.error) h += banner("blocked", "Not verified", "<div>" + esc(r.error) + "</div>");\n    h += 
kv(Object.keys(r).filter((k) => !["citation"].includes(k)).map((k) => [k, typeof r[k] === "object" ? 
JSON.stringify(r[k]) : r[k]]));\n    if (r.response_hash) h += "<p class=\\"muted\\">Retrieval audit: sha256 " + 
esc(r.response_hash) + " at " + esc(txt(r.retrieved_at)) + "</p>";\n    out.innerHTML = h;\n  } catch (e) { 
out.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); }\n}\n\nasync function getCitation() {\n  const out = $("#refout");\n  try {\n    
const r = await post("/api/literature/verify", { title: $("#reftitle").value, doi: $("#refdoi").value,\n      source: 
$("#refsrc").value, cite: $("#refstyle").value });\n    state.ref = r;\n    let h = "";\n    if (r.citation) h += 
\'<pre class="json">\' + esc(r.citation.text || r.citation) + "</pre>";\n    if (r.citation && r.citation.notice) h +=

"<div>" + esc(r.error) + "</div>");\n    out.innerHTML = h || \'<p class="muted">The server returned no 
citation.</p>\';\n  } catch (e) { out.innerHTML = errorBanner(e); }\n}\n\nasync function showLog() {\n  const out = 
$("#reflog");\n  out.innerHTML = loading("Reading the retrieval log");\n  try {\n    const r = await

await api("/api/literature/drift");\n    const rows = r.results || r.entries || [];\n    out.innerHTML = \'<div

places: 2,\n  chart: [\n    { code: "1000", name: "Cash", type: "asset", line: "Cash", role: "cash" },\n    { code:

account: "3000", credit: "1000" }] }],\n  entries: [{ date: "2026-02-01", description: "capital introduced",\n         
     lines: [{ account: "1000", debit: "500" }, { account: "3000", credit: "500" }] }],\n  schedules: []\n};\nlet 
checkJournal = null;\n\nfunction parseTrialBalance(text) {\n  const rows = [], problems = [], numeric = (v) => v === 
"" || /^-?\\d+(\\.\\d+)?$/.test(v);\n  String(text || "").split(/\\r?\\n/).forEach((line, i) => {\n    if 
(!line.trim()) return;\n    const cells = line.split(/\\t|;|,(?=(?:[^"]*"[^"]*")*[^"]*$)/).map((x) => 
x.replace(/^"|"$/g, "").trim());\n    if (cells.length < 3) { problems.push("Line " + (i + 1) + ": needs three columns 
- account, debit, credit."); return; }\n    const name = cells[0], debit = cells[1].replace(/[,\\s]/g, ""), credit = 
cells[2].replace(/[,\\s]/g, "");\n    if (!numeric(debit) || !numeric(credit)) {\n      if (i === 0 && 
/account|debit|credit/i.test(cells.join(" "))) return;      // a header row is not an error\n      problems.push("Line 
" + (i + 1) + ": the debit and credit must be numbers, or blank.");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!name) { 
problems.push("Line " + (i + 1) + ": the account name is blank."); return; }\n    rows.push({ name: name, debit: debit 
|| "0", credit: credit || "0" });\n  });\n  return { rows: rows, problems: problems };\n}\n\nfunction checkMoney(v) { 
return v === null || v === undefined ? "" : esc(String(v)); }\n\nfunction checkReport(d, det) {\n  const verdict = 
det.verdict || (d.delivery && d.delivery.report) || "";\n  let html = \'<div class="card"><h2>The verdict</h2><p>\' +

esc(f.detail) + "</li>").join("") + "</ul>";\n  }\n  html += "</div>";\n  if (det.rows && det.rows.length) {\n    html 
+= \'<div class="card"><h2>Line by line</h2><table><thead><tr><th>Account</th><th>Your figure</th>\' +\n      
"<th>From your journal</th><th>Difference</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>" + det.rows.map((r) =>\n      "<tr><td>" + 
esc(r.name || r.code || "") + "</td><td>" + checkMoney(r.submitted) + "</td><td>" +\n      checkMoney(r.rebuilt) + 
"</td><td>" + checkMoney(r.difference) + "</td><td>" + esc(r.status) + "</td></tr>").join("") +\n
